| 03:5-7 | Vessel issue: after the main vessel dies, F selects a minion, not the main vessel after respawn (Boutman/Boutbro, maybe others). Fix: built into the pack's shared code and runs with Argo, Yossele, Spool and Uloe. | `Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:21-77 (Update installs After on the Focus action; After selects and moves MainHero to the front of Entityclienttags['Hero'], lines 56-70); Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:80-95 (MainHero finds the local player's own vessel)` | MATCH | The shared fix does what the line says. The four vessel parts' call sites are not in the files given, so 'runs with Argo, Yossele, Spool and Uloe' is not checked here. Line 6 is the how-to-see test, not a build line; its reproduction is covered by the same code. |
| 03:8 | Status: built; the PC pass on a real death and respawn ran with the fix not yet switched on; fixed since and compiled, so the next PC round checks it again. | `Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:44-54 (Respawned set when the local vessel object changes); Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:56-70 (After acts only when Respawned is set)` | MATCH | The reworked detection (a changed vessel reference, kit 1.11 comment at lines 7-9) is in the file. The PC re-check is an open item outside the code. |
| 03:10-13 | No way back in: a player who loses the connection mid-map cannot return because the lobby is shut for the whole map. Fix: the host keeps each player's seat (vessel, towers, aura), opens the lobby while a seat is empty, and the dropped player returns with Join Game in Steam. Rejoining must not interrupt the others. | `09_Rejoin.cs:59-98 (Host: seats kept per session at map start, Copy of vessel/team/aura/stats); 09_Rejoin.cs:100-127 (a returning session gets Restore, seat given back); 09_Rejoin.cs:138 (message to use Join Game in Steam); 09_Rejoin.cs:144-147 (lobby opened while a seat is empty, shut when all are back); 09_Rejoin.cs:189-202 (TurnAway: anyone not in the map is kicked, so others are not disturbed); 09_Rejoin.cs:153-160 (Door)` | MATCH | The seat, the open lobby, the Join Game message and the non-interruption rule are built. Towers are kept by the game's own reconnect (09_Rejoin.cs header lines 7-8), not by the pack; the code relies on that and does not build it. |
| 03:14 | Status: built, passed on the PC with a real second player including a game restart; Steam's Join Game and a real network's drop wait for the online evening. | `10_Online.cs:6-62 (RWIdentity: keeps the player's GUID in the pack's save so a restarted friend keeps their Sessionid, Update lines 20-62); 09_Rejoin.cs:59-148 (seat logic)` | MATCH | The restart-rejoin code is present. The PC pass and the Steam Join Game and network-drop checks are verification outside the code (the online evening, RESEARCH_GAPS section E). |

The scope says all towers. |

## 3. Design pages against code

Each vessel's `DESIGN.md` (lines as D:N), every number and rule mapped to its code. The scope wins over a design page: a DIFFERENT row is fixed in whichever of the two the scope agrees with least.

| File | MATCH | DIFFERENT | NOT-IN-CODE |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Uloe/DESIGN.md | 133 | 4 | 5 |

| File | Lines | Says | Code | Mark | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Uloe/DESIGN.md | D:34 | Health regen 0 | `Uloe/Code/02_Data.cs:72-75` | NOT-IN-CODE | No Regen stat appears in HeroStats or BuildHero; searched for Regen and Healthregen. The template's value is inherited. |
| Uloe/DESIGN.md | D:52 | Unlocked from the start | `Uloe/Code/04_Content.cs:385-401; Uloe/Code/06_HeroBrain.cs:93-98` | NOT-IN-CODE | No unlock or gating check; the command is created in Begin with no condition. |
| Uloe/DESIGN.md | D:52 | Cost none | `Uloe/Code/04_Content.cs:385-401` | NOT-IN-CODE | The Command function sets no Costs; the template's values are inherited, not set to none. |
| Uloe/DESIGN.md | D:59 | 4, or 6 with Short Circuit | `Uloe/Code/06_HeroBrain.cs:162; Uloe/Code/07_Building.cs:19` | DIFFERENT | UpgradeCount counts every upgrade held, not Short Circuit alone. Any single upgrade gives 6, for example Extra Cell. |
| Uloe/DESIGN.md | D:100-101 | Skin bands above Light 0, 4 and 8 | `Uloe/Code/02_Data.cs:86; Uloe/Code/07_Building.cs:135-147` | DIFFERENT | Thresholds 0, 4, 8 are used: the code takes band i+1 when raw is at least 4 or 8, not strictly above, so exactly 4 or 8 shows the higher band. |

| Uloe/DESIGN.md | D:327-331 | Tests 8b cover the 24 upgrades in Code/98_Upgrades.cs | `Uloe/Code/02_Data.cs:199-313` | NOT-IN-CODE | No Code/98_Upgrades.cs file is in the pack; the upgrade definitions are in 02_Data.cs. |
